Document 010: 11月2日两场座谈会点评：小资金大乘数：稳市工具制度化如何改变定价分母

Source PDF: all report pdf/11月2日两场座谈会点评：小资金大乘数：稳市工具制度化如何改变定价分母.pdf
Report source: 山西证券
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

——11 月 2 日两场座谈会点评
2026 年 11 月 3 日 策略研究/事件点评
相关报告：
山证策略团队
分析师：
赵晨希
执业登记编码：S0760521090001
邮箱：zhaochenxi@sxzq.com
事件描述：
 两场座谈会同步落地：央行、金监总局、证监会于 11 月 2 日先后召开
“证券、基金、保险公司互换便利座谈会”和“股票回购增持再贷款座
谈会”。证监会副主席刘浩凌、央行副行长邹澜、金监总局副局长丛林
出席，23 家证券基金公司、26 家银行参会。
 两项工具“从应急走向制度化”：（1）互换便利（SFISF）：余额 1050
亿元，第三批扩容至 31 家券商/基金公司，申请额度超 1000 亿元，11
月 3 日第三次 SFISF 操作实际落地 1000 亿元，31 家机构参与，中标费
率 10bp，期限 1 年可展期，后续将“适当增加参与机构、降低互换费率、
优化质押模式”。（2）回购增持再贷款：签约约 4400 亿、累计发放超
2800 亿；由阶段性安排转为长期制度性安排，再贷款利率从 1.25%降至

1%。
 证监会答记者问的四条政策线：（1）工具端：引导权重指数公司、龙头
科技股做出标志性回购案例，督促已披露计划加速实施。（2）产品端：
18 只主动 ETF 募资超 350 亿（16 只触达 20 亿上限），加快建仓；继续
推权益类 ETF、股债恒定比例多资产指数基金、浮动费率基金。（3）两
融端：研究优化融资保证金比例、折算率、维持担保比例，适度释放融
资空间，盘活存量流动性。（4）回购规则：要求年度分红划出比例纳入
回购专项基金、超募资金强制部分回购、护盘式回购收益闭环用于注销。
投资要点：定价机制改变，而非资金本身。政策通过降低回购成本、提供机
构流动性支持、优化杠杆管理参数三条路径，系统性降低了权益资产的持有
成本和交易摩擦。政策影响的量化特征呈现“小资金、大乘数”结构，三项
工具可动用资金规模占自由流通市值仅约 1.42%，但通过成本压降、EPS 增
厚、PE 扩张三条乘数路径，潜在影响规模可达数万亿级别，同时两融松绑
作为弹性增强器可改善存量资金的使用效率，提升短线风险偏好。

 规模维度：政策工具属催化剂而非主要增量资金。政策工具来看，互换
便利余额 2050 亿元，占 A 股自由流通市值约 0.43%；回购增持再贷款
签约约 4400 亿，占 A 股自由流通市值约 0.92%，其中累计发放超 2800
亿，占 A 股自由流通市值约 0.58%；主动 ETF 募资 350 亿元，占 A 股
自由流通市值约 0.07%。三项工具可动用规模合计约 6,800 亿元，占全
A 流通市值约 1.42%。量级上可见政策工具并非可直接托举指数的体量，
而是通过压低风险溢价和交易成本来撬动市场自身定价，属于催化剂而
非主要增量资金投放。
 成本维度：再贷款降息的财务影响。再贷款利率从 1.25%降至 1%，存
量 2800 亿对应年利息成本从 35 亿元降至 28 亿元，年省 7 亿元，降幅
20%；若增量 1600 亿（签约 4400 亿元−已发 2800 亿元）全部按 1%投

### Page 2

放，年成本仅 16 亿元。4400 亿元全量按 1%，年总成本 44 亿元，比旧
利率省 11 亿元/年。对单家回购公司，成本下降直接转化为回购的净收
益，1%的利率已低于多数高分红公司的股息率，回购注销的套利空间
从接近中性变为明确正收益。
 盈利维度：回购注销对 EPS 的增厚。用公式 EPS 增厚 ≈ 回购额/市值
÷ (1−回购额/市值) 测算，假设温和情景回购占市值比为 0.6%（略高于
2800 亿元占全 A 市值比）、中性情景回购占市值比为 1%、积极情景回
购占市值比为 1.4%，EPS 增厚分别约 0.60%、1.01%、1.42%，回购额
占全 A 归母总净利（假设 Q3 线推）比分别约为 8.78%、14.63%、20.48%，
回购额整体不超过年利润，并未构成对存量现金的消耗，高 ROE 和强
现金流的公司为这一机制的受益者。
 杠杆维度：两融保证金比例调整的弹性。从保证金比例、折算率、维持
担保比例三个维度同时研究优化，表明监管层有意释放存量融资空间的
效率。融资保证金比例下调后，存量保证金可撬动千亿资金。以 2026

年 10 月 30 日实际融资余额 23078.12 亿元为基准，按监管通用公式推导，
若保证金比例从当前的 100%下调至新比例 95%、90%、80%，理论增
量分别可达 1214.64 亿元、2564.24 亿元、4072.61 亿元，考虑参与率、
风险偏好、集中度等因素，实际增量亦可达百亿元，潜在杠杆资金是短
期弹性的主要来源，也是波动放大的潜在根源。
 机构维度：互换便利的扩容效应。叠加存量展期余额 1050 亿元，全市
场可参考的互换便利支持池约 2050 亿量级。31 家机构分享 1000 亿新增
申请，均值约 32.3 亿元/家，若全额操作，占全 A 日均成交额（假设积
极情景约 1.8 万亿）的 5.56%，10bp 互换费率下，1000 亿年互换费用仅
约 1 亿元，利润影响可忽略，信号意义远大于成本意义，工具已完成从
应急到常态化的定位切换。单家 32.3 亿元的流动性额度，对头部券商而
言是“低成本流动性保险”， 对中型机构是打破融资分层的关键通道。
机构维度的价值不在利润弹性，而在降低系统性被动抛售概率。

 估值维度：盈利底与政策底的乘数效应。政策工具通过压缩流动性折价、
治理不确定折价、消除尾部风险预期压缩分母端风险溢价，撬动整个市
场估值中枢上移的乘数效应。全 A 总市值 116.45 万亿，三季报全 A 归
母净利润 5.10 万亿，隐含 PE 14.63 倍，PE 每扩张 1 倍，对应市值增量
7.96 万亿，相当于当前总市值的 6.83%。这是最关键的量化锚点：政策
工具压缩的是风险溢价，直接体现为 PE 扩张，14.63 倍的隐含 PE 为这
一扩张提供了估值空间，而 7.96 万亿的每倍 PE 弹性说明政策的边际影
响在估值端远大于在资金端。
风险提示：政策执行不及预期；盈利下修对冲估值扩张的风险；海外流动性
收紧与外资风险偏好外溢；两融杠杆双向放大与结构性脆弱；回购注销执行
质量与制度套利风险；盈利预期与政策工具落地节奏潜在乘数效应影响等。

7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。

美期中選舉牽動市場表現，股債配置重品質、控風險
2026 年 11 月 04 日
一、 市場動態
1. 美期中選舉牽動市場情緒，亞股一度隨美股期貨下挫：美國 11 月 3 日舉行期中選舉
，根據 CNN 網站的結果顯示，截至台北時間 11/4 下午 3 點左右，民主黨於參議院
已取得 100 席中的 53 席的過半席次，於眾議院則在 435 席中取得了 225 席(218 席
過半)。市場重新評估國會控制權及川普政府未來政策推動能力，擔心民主黨國會影響
力擴大 ，可能對財政政策及科技企業監管形成新的制衡 。S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100

期貨在開票期間一度下挫 1.38%及 2.35%，科技股明顯承受較大賣壓。惟隨後跌深反
彈，截至台北時間下午 3 時左右，S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100 期貨跌幅已收斂至
0.22%及 0.44%。亞洲市場方面，科技股為主及出口導向股市受到美股期貨拖累，台
灣加權指數 11/4 盤中最低下挫 3.38%，所幸隨跌幅收斂，終場下跌 0.83%；韓股同
日也由大跌近 4%由黑翻紅上漲 0.62%。11/3 未開盤的日股，在 11/4 開盤後出現補
跌，最低下挫近 4.8%，但最後跌幅收斂至下跌 1.42%。
2. 美國公債及美元同步反映政策預期調整，市場避險情緒升溫：美國公債及美元反映政
策預期調整，殖利率盤中先跌後漲：10 年期公債殖利率盤中先跌後漲，截至台北時間
下午 3 時來到 4.906%；同一時間，美元指數小跌約 0.37%。股、債、匯市的變動顯
示，投資人除了調整股票風險部位，也在評估未來美國財政刺激規模、政府債務供給
與利率前景。
二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨取得國會控制權，市場重新定價財政與監管政策：本次選舉對金融市場的主要

影響，在於未來行政與立法部門的權力分配可能改變。共和黨先前同時掌握白宮與國
會，有利於推動稅制、財政及產業政策；民主黨在參眾兩院均取得過半席次後，部分

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
政策將面臨更高的協商成本。財政方面，新增減稅及擴大支出的空間可能受到限制，
市場因而下修部分財政刺激預期；企業方面，國會監督權可能增加對大型科技企業、
AI 資料中心、反壟斷及資料隱私議題的調查壓力。惟國會控制權改變不代表既有政策
立即逆轉，也不代表所有監管措施均能完成立法。此次市場反應更可能是投資人重新
要求政策風險溢酬，而非企業獲利已發生實質下修。
2. AI 資本支出與投資回報疑慮尚未消退，放大科技股對政治事件的反應：此次選舉開票
期間，Nasdaq 期貨及與科技相關的亞股出現大幅波動，亦與近期科技股基本面及評

價爭議有關。10 月底以來美股因大型科技企業 AI 投資效益受到質疑而大幅修正，市
場關注焦點逐漸由 AI 基礎建設支出規模 ， 轉向相關投資能否創造足夠的營運現金流與
股東報酬。以雲端服務及資料中心產業而言，企業雖可透過AI 運算需求增加帶動營收
成長，但 GPU、伺服器、電力設備及資料中心建設需要龐大前期資金，若營收回收速
度不如預期，可能使自由現金流承壓並增加外部融資需求。近期部分科技企業籌資計
畫及財測不佳，已使投資人提高對財務品質的要求。當市場原先已對AI 投資回報存有
疑慮，新的政治及監管不確定性便更容易放大評價修正幅度。
三、 市場看法
1. 半導體需求仍具分化 ， 政治事件不等於AI 產業成長趨勢反轉 ：科技股近期的股價修正
與 AI 終端需求惡化並非相同概念。AMD 近期公布優於市場預期的財報，並對資料中
心業務提出較強勁的成長展望，顯示 AI 運算需求仍具有一定韌性；相對而言，Intel
先前財測疲弱及現金流壓力，則反映不同半導體企業的產品競爭力、資本效率及終端
市場曝險存在明顯差異。因此，不宜將半導體股整體下跌解讀為AI 需求全面降溫。從

產業鏈觀察，具備技術領先優勢、穩定客戶需求及良好現金流的企業，與高度依賴外
部融資、尚未形成穩定獲利模式的基礎建設業者，對資金成本及監管變化的承受能力
並不相同。未來市場可能更加重視個別企業的獲利品質，而非單純以AI 相關營收成長
作為估值依據。
2. 政治事件可能延長科技股評價調整，但中期走勢仍取決於企業獲利：本次期中選舉引
發的期貨跌勢，反映市場對政策方向及國會控制權變化的即時反應，惟政治不確定性
通常須透過實際立法、企業投資計畫或經濟活動變化，才會形成較持久的基本面影響
。從企業獲利角度觀察，路透社引用 LSEG 資料指出，S&P 500 第三季企業獲利仍呈
現強勁成長，科技企業對整體獲利貢獻顯著，顯示目前尚無充分證據支持美國企業基
本面全面轉弱。不過，科技股先前已累積較高成長預期，市場對資本支出回報及監管
風險的容忍度降低，可能使股價即使在營收成長下仍承受評價壓力。短期而言，若選
舉結果逐漸明朗，且企業獲利預估未進一步下修，市場有機會逐步消化政治風險；反
之，若政策不確定性伴隨獲利預期下修，修正時間可能延長。

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：根據彭博統計，1950
年以來美國歷次期中選舉後 12 個月，S&P 500 平均報酬率約 15%，且 19 次全數
收正。其中最高為 1954 年的逾 30%，表現最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。我們認為
，選前政策不確定性消除加上府會分治抑制財政與監管的轉向是為歷史上選後行情的
主要支撐，但相關數據所呈現的為過往經驗，未來股市表現能否延續先前情況仍應觀
察後續基本面變化。
4. 美國公債具避險價值，但利率下行能否持續仍取決於通膨與財政前景：在美國期中選

舉開票期間，美股期貨下跌伴隨美債殖利率回落，顯示公債仍能在股市波動期間提供
一定的風險分散效果。然而，本次殖利率下降可能同時包含避險買盤、財政刺激預期
下修及市場對經濟成長前景重新評估等因素，不宜直接解讀為聯準會即將轉向寬鬆。
尤其能源市場仍受到中東地緣政治及供應中斷風險影響，油價波動可能使通膨前景反
覆，限制長天期公債殖利率的下行空間。若未來財政擴張受到限制，可能對長端利率
形成部分支撐；但若政治僵局提高債務上限協商風險，亦可能使美債期限溢酬波動。
因此，債券配置仍須同時考量收益率、存續期間與通膨風險，不宜僅根據單日行情決
定配置方向。
5. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：目前科技股修正主
要反映評價與政策風險，但若AI 企業持續增加資本支出及外部融資，市場對債務負擔
與償債能力的關注可能進一步升高。對大型投資等級科技企業而言，較穩定的營運現
金流及融資管道有助於吸收資本支出壓力；但對依賴單一客戶合約、設備抵押融資或
高槓桿經營的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 利率與信用利差上升可能更直接影響投資回報及再融

資能力。後續應追蹤投資等級債與高收益債 OAS、科技及雲端相關發行人利差、新債
發行需求及信用評等展望。若科技股下跌但整體信用利差維持穩定，市場風險可能仍
以股權評價調整為主；若信用利差同步持續擴大，則須提高對金融條件收緊及風險跨
市場擴散的警覺。

### Page 4

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
四、 投資建議：
1. 科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，惟短線宜審慎布局並重視個股分化：美國期中選舉帶
來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，惟大型雲端業者持續投入AI 基礎建
設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，目前尚不宜因美股期貨在開票期間的
下挫即全面轉向保守。我們仍建議維持美股核心配置，但短期避免過度集中於高評價
、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企業。產業方面，資訊科技維持選擇性配置，優
先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業；對雲端基
礎建設及 AI 融資相關標的，則應提高財務結構與現金流審查標準。

2. 維持中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債配置，兼顧收益與風險分散：在美國政策風
險升溫及科技股評價調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源
價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延長存續期間。建議以中天期美國公債及高信評投資
等級債作為主要配置方向，兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置穩定性。信用債方
面，建議優先選擇營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人，對AI 相關
企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD震盪下跌。週初，EUR/USD在 1.1200整數關卡窄幅波動；週間
受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22  
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公

### Page 3

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas
customers. Management emphasized that SOFC shipments will remain limited

4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

The legal entities through which Citi conducts its business do not permit investment research recommendations, ratings,

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Positive news on AI ASIC
execution and project win;
reiterate OW
We expect the stock to react positively to the AI ASIC
developments; OW: 1) TPU v9 on schedule, 2) confidence in Intel
EMIB-T substrate yield improvement, and 3) second customer
win. We maintain our  PT of NT$6,188 and Top Pick.
What's new? MediaTek confirms second customer win: In our preview note,  we
had expected some discussion on a potential second customer. Today, management

confirmed the win, and indicated it is not a China CSP customer, hence, we think it
could be a US CSP  customer. We are modeling US$2.5bn revenue from the second
customer in 2028, but management thinks the demand should be higher than that.
Status of major TPU projects - on track: TPU v8 expected to contribute US$2.5bn
revenue in 4Q26 (vs. prior guidance of >US$2bn). Major AI ASIC customer’s TPU v9
is expected to tape out on time and starts mass production in 1Q28, dismissing

recent delay concerns. While TPU v9 volume highly depends on Intel EMIB-T
substrate supply, management has confidence in EMIB-T yield improvement. The
company is also working closely with the customer on the third generation
(TPUv10) and will disclose more details at the appropriate time.
Our key question to management:  Will both major  CSP customers prioritize GPU
or ASIC, if/when we see cloud capex budget shrinkage? Management responded
that, while not every ASIC in the industry can compete with GPU, the ASICs they

help to design (TPUv8 and v9) enjoy very competitive TCO (total cost of
ownership). Thus, we believe MediaTek’s AI ASIC revenue can track our forecast, if
the substrate supply is sufficient.
4Q26 revenue to grow 25-32% Q/Q, with operating margin improvement thanks
to AI ASIC: Gross margin guidance of 45% is slightly better than our 44%-45%,
given some price hikes for smartphone and smart-edge products. After deducting
the US$2.5bn revenue contribution from the AI ASIC in the quarter, the non-AI

business should be down 17% Q/Q.
We think the implied AI ASIC operating margin could be 25%-30% at scale: This
assumes AI ASIC is 35% of 4Q26 revenue and the company improves its 4Q26
operating margin to 19% (up from 16.1% in 3Q26),

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside
n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.

n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Near-term weakness already in the price; maintain Buy and lower TP to NT$286
- Despite near-term margin pressure from softer-than-expected quarterly revenue
and rising depreciation cost, electricity and OPEX, management sees GM
expansion into next year supported by stronger utilization, operating leverage, and
favorable AI-related product mix, in particular substantial upside from wafer

sorting. We lower our earnings projection for 2026/2027 /2028 by 6%/17%/15%
reflecting near-term GM shortfall and potential increasing depreciation cost.
Considering remaining strong AI cycle, we keep our Buy rating but lower our DCF-
derived TP to NT$286 given our lower estimates and concerns on recent
macro/policy uncertainty. Our new TP of NT$286 implies 25x/17x of our 2027 /28
EPS estimates.

### Page 6

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)
06 November 2026 Citi Research
6
King Yuan Electronics Co
Company description
Founded in 1987, King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) mainly provides testing
services for the back-end process of semiconductor production globally. With
more than 30 years of industry experience, KYEC has built up firm
relationships with several leading global IC design/IDM customers. Its
headquarters and production facilities are both located in Taiwan, with sales

offices in North America, Japan, and Singapore.
Investment strategy
We rate KYEC as Buy. As a key testing service provider for AI GPU and ASIC
products, KYEC is well positioned to benefit from the significant AI growth
opportunity in the years to come. Currently, KYEC is the major final testing
service provider for its major customer's AI GPU, and we expect KYEC to
continue to secure its dominant share in its customer's upcoming AI GPU
product lines due to its specialty in burn-in testing, smooth product delivery

capability, and its strong business partnership with its customer. We are also
positive over the growth outlook of its smartphone business thanks to the
integration of AI functionality, leading to a better replacement cycle. We
believe KYEC's major customer in smartphone will benefit from an improved
product mix, which will result in longer testing times for the flagship SoC.
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology.

Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of 4%, a risk premium of 5%,
and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a
3.0% interest rate on its debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume
terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually
improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares
would trade at 2027 /28E P/B of 4x/4x and 2027 /28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line
with its global peers.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-demand for global
smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related
products; 3) slower-than-expected demand recovery in the automotive and
base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 1

WT Microelectronics

Datacenter continues to fuel growth amid 
a less favorable funding surrounding

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 270.00 TWD | Price: 195.00 TWD

3Q26 ahead on operating leverage  
3Q26 net income NT$10.3bn was 4% ahead of BofAe/consensus, thanks mainly to a 
slightly stronger-than-expected operating leverage. By end markets, the sequential 
revenue momentum turned out to be stronger-than-expected across auto, industrial, CE 
(consumer electronics), PC (personal computer) and smartphone verticals in 3Q26, due 
partly to the earlier pull-in which also will likely lead to a QoQ revenue drop in 4Q26 for 
these segments.  
Reaccelerating datacenter business into 4Q26 and 2027 
4Q26 revenue is guided to grow by 25+% QoQ based on the mid-point of guidance 
range, thanks mainly to the reacceleration in datacenter-related business, which more 
than offset the QoQ revenue decline in the segments mentioned above. Yet we expect 
the firm’s interest expense to notably increase in 4Q26 (vs 3Q26) to NT$3bn+, while 
management also attributes such increase to a higher funding cost (interest rate) 
environment and a bigger amount of working capital required to fuel future business 
growth. Looking into 2027, WT Micro expects continuous growth across datacenter, auto 
and industrial areas.   
Lower PO on a lowered multiple given higher interest rate 
We raise 2026-28E EPS by 7-11% after baking in the assumptions of stronger operating 
leverage post the release of 3Q26/4Q26 results/guidance, while our PO goes to NT$270 
(from NT$330) now based on 7x (was 10x) 2H27-1H28E P/E, at the low-to-mid-end of 
its historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x, we believe this level of PE is warranted 
because of a higher funding cost (interest rate at 4+%) environment into 2027-28 
balanced by a growing op margin, ROE and EPS CAGR over the forecast period. Given 
the still undemanding valuation at 6x/5x P/E and 11%/14% dividend yield in 2027/28E, 
we reiterate Buy rating on the stock, while staying confident for the company to record 
20+% top-line/bottom-line CAGR during 2026-28E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
WT Microelectronics was founded in 1993 and went listed 
in 2002. The firm is the biggest distributor for 
semiconductor and technology hardware components 
globally with 10+% market share, and with more than 25k 
customers and over 400 suppliers. WT Micro operates in 
dual-headquarter business model, after acquiring Future 
Electronics in 2024, and now it has over eight thousand 
employees with over 160 sales branches.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on WT Micro, as we like the structural 
growth in datacenter business mainly driven by TPU, as well 
as the emerging benefits/synergies from the cross-selling 
with Future Electronics.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
WT Microelectronics (XZOPF) 
We base our PO NT$270 on 7x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at the low-to-mid-end of its 
historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x. This is justified in our view, given the higher 
interest rate balanced by the support from: 1) an operating margin of 2.2-2.3% in 2027-
28E, 2) improving ROE toward 33% in 2027-28E (vs 12% in 2024-25), and 3) 22% EPS 
CAGR during 2026-28E.

Upside risks to our PO are: 
1) faster-than-expected demand recovery in auto/industrial verticals, especially from 
US/Europe market 
2) a lower interest rate that could alleviate the company's financial burden at non-
operating level.

Downside risks to our PO are: 
1) a revenue decline in datacenter/server related business, which has been the key 
growth driver during 2021-24 
2) market share loss as suppliers or customers seek other distributors' help after WT 
Micro consolidates Future Electronics. 
3) Potential overhang from share dilution.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

rating)

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry,

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0.pdf

Date: 2026-10-30

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review]  매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PP A 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PP A 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR은 2035년 운전을 목표로 2028년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031년 출하 물량은 2028년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%) 
ㆍSTRONG BUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +50%이상 3% 
ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3% 
ㆍREDUCE(매도) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%미만 0%

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV   Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV

### Page 2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

Document 323: Democratic victories to constrain President Trump and increase tech scrutiny

### Page 1

The opinion polls called it right with sweeping victories for the
Democrats in the midterm elections. The key question is
whether the president will look to secure deals or whether
gridlock on the domestic front will force him to look outwards

James Knightley, Carsten Brzeski, Padhraic Garvey, CFA, Lynn
Song and Chris Turner
Rep. Katherine Clark, the Democratic House minority whip, House Democratic
Leader Hakeem Jeﬀries, and Democratic Congressional Campaign Committee
Chair Suzan DelBene
The results are in and it's a big Democratic
victory
With most votes counted, it's clear the Democrats have secured a
decisive victory in the US midterms, claiming majorities in both the
Senate and House of Representatives. Though this outcome had
become the consensus in recent weeks, the scale of the Democratic
victory in the Senate in particular is noteworthy. Wins in Texas, but
also in less expected states like South Carolina, Kansas and Iowa,

US midterm results (as of 06:00 ET)
Source: New York Times, ING
Dealmaker-in-Chief or Mr Veto?
The Republican majorities in the House and Senate in the ﬁrst half
of Trump's presidential term allowed him to sign the One Big
Beautiful Bill Act into law in July 2025. Other legislation included
the Laken Riley Act, which tightened immigration and border policy,
the GENIUS Act, which created a regulatory structure for

stablecoins, the HALT Fentanyl Act on drugs and the TAKE IT DOWN
Act on online regulation.

### Page 3

The obvious outcome from the result on Tuesday is that a
Democrat-controlled Congress will stiﬂe President Trump’s domestic
agenda in his ﬁnal two years in power. A bill has to be approved by
both the House and the Senate before being passed on to the
president to sign or veto. With the Democrats having control of the
legislative agenda in the House and Senate, the president has
already warned that he will likely become “Mr Veto”. The only way
to overcome that would be for the Democrats to secure a two-thirds

supermajority in both houses. That isn't the case after last night's
results.
The consequence is that spending and tax proposals, as with most
domestic legislation, will likely be gridlocked. There are potential
areas of compromise tied to healthcare aﬀordability in exchange
for perhaps more spending on defence, but major tax and spending
changes appear unlikely.
On the one hand, this is often viewed positively, as it provides a
stable backdrop for corporate America. On the other, it means there

is little prospect of meaningful change to the nation’s ﬁscal stance.
While we won’t see any further ﬁscal expansion, large ﬁscal deﬁcits
and deteriorating debt dynamics will continue.
The ﬁscal situation will certainly remain a concern, although hitting
the debt ceiling could act as a temporary brake. The issue is likely
to come to a head by mid-summer 2027, with little scope for
compromise between the two sides in Congress. This risks an
extended government shutdown, potentially exceeding last

October’s shutdown, which depressed GDP growth in 4Q 2025 by a
full percentage point. The Federal Reserve risks “ﬂying blind” again
with no government economic statistics being collated or released.
What the Democrats can seek to achieve
Key sectors of corporate America will be nervous that Democratic
control of important oversight committees could bring greater
scrutiny. More congressional hearings involving AI and technology
business leaders would be a likely outcome, while a strong

performance at the ballot box may embolden local Democratic

### Page 4

leaders to try to stall unpopular data centre construction in their
constituencies. Fossil fuel producers may face similar challenges.
Democrats will likely feel even more conﬁdent about pushing back
against President Trump’s policies on Iran, support of Israel and any
escalation of tariﬀs. They may apply more pressure on the
president to sound tougher towards Russia and to provide support
to Ukraine, but the ﬁnal call will remain with the president.

Investigations into President Trump, his family and members of his
administration may also be stepped up as Democrats exercise their
greater powers. Inquiries into ﬁnances and business dealings could
result in subpoenas for documents and testimony. Moreover,
impeachment proceedings begin in the House, raising the
possibility of a third attempt to impeach the president.
To be convicted and removed from oﬃce, 67 of the 100 senators
would need to vote in favour, which is unlikely. The Democrats could

still use the process to constrain the president, but doing so risks
further toxifying the political situation in the US. Rather than
pursuing such action against the president himself, Democrats
could instead launch investigations into members of his cabinet.
With Democrats now in control of the Senate, they can block or, at
the very least, stall judicial and executive nominees. This includes
heads of government agencies, ambassadors and cabinet

members, potentially making it diﬃcult for the president to replace
any senior oﬃcials he chooses to ﬁre.
What will the president do?
A key question is whether a constrained President Trump, entering
the ﬁnal phase of his tenure, becomes more willing to accept
political or market disruption in pursuit of his objectives.
If there is little sense of any compromise, the ensuing gridlock in
Congress would leave the president to focus on areas where he can

exercise executive power: 1) foreign policy and diplomacy, including
trade policy, 2) national security and defence, 3) law enforcement
and regulatory policy, 4) personnel appointments (subject to
Senate approval), and 5) administration and management policy.

### Page 5

This suggests Iran, Russia/Ukraine and tariﬀs/trade policy are likely
to be the focus for the president. Tariﬀ policy could increasingly be
used to incentivise foreign partners to align with US foreign policy
objectives, for example by pressuring NATO members to increase
defence spending.
More fundamentally, heavy defeats in the House and Senate will be
interpreted as a referendum on President Trump’s policies and
potentially prompt ﬁgures within the Republican Party to take the

view that it is time to open the debate on its future direction. With
Trump’s inﬂuence set to fade, leading lights within the party, most
notably JD Vance and Marco Rubio, may attempt to shore up
support for their own campaigns as they look to the 2028
presidential election. Similarly, a large Democratic victory will
intensify speculation over whether the party’s 2028 candidate
should come from its more moderate wing or whether the
progressive left has enough momentum to put forward a candidate

of its own.
The market reaction
The initial bond market reaction has been a fall in yields across the
curve. In the coming days, that could reverse as the prospect of
policy stasis leaves the market weighing a still-elevated ﬁscal
deﬁcit against some easing of inﬂation concerns. We will probably
see front-end yields drift lower, while the back end retains an upside
bias. If we do see more tariﬀs, the back end could ﬁnd a small silver
lining in the additional tax revenues. But any beneﬁt is likely to be

marginal, given the risk that tariﬀs also push inﬂation higher.
Overall, we retain a bias towards the 10-year yield heading towards
5.25%.
The dollar is softer on the outcome, though it has recovered from its
lows seen in early Asia when a sharp fall in Nasdaq futures was
warning of a larger re-pricing of the tech story. Nasdaq futures have
since retraced the majority of those losses. Limiting a larger dollar
correction on this news is probably a still-resilient US economy and

at least one more rate hike from the Federal Reserve. The dollar
could see a little more weakness into 2027, however, if an

### Page 6

emboldened Democratic Party takes greater issue with Republican
policy, including deregulation and the AI build-out. And the
potential for a prolonged government shutdown in mid-2027 adds
to the case for a weaker dollar next summer.
What it means for Europe and China
With the outcome of the vote limiting President Trump’s options at
home, he is likely to focus more heavily on foreign and trade policy.
The result could be greater volatility and uncertainty for the global

economy.
This will clearly aﬀect Europe. A forced deal between Russia and
Ukraine, to the disadvantage of Ukraine and Europe, could
undermine economic sentiment in Europe. A new escalation in the
Middle East would push up energy prices and harm the European
economy. Europe’s latest shift towards more protectionist trade
policies could bring new global trade tensions.
At the same time, with only two years left in oﬃce, the TACO
(Trump Always Chickens Out) phenomenon could increasingly

shape Europe’s thinking. The risk of an unwarranted economic
accident rises as Trump’s policies towards Europe become more
aggressive and Europe’s own stance hardens, in the hope that
Trump will ultimately back down or simply run out of time.
If Republicans lay the blame for the poor result at the president’s
door, the sense that Trump’s time is coming to an end could grow
stronger. If so, some European countries may be tempted to test
the president’s patience and slow the pace of defence spending

increases, particularly given their own ﬁscal constraints.
Alternatively, and less likely, a stiﬂed but less angry Trump
administration could try to bring the wars in Iran and Ukraine to an
end. Constrained by Congress and the Supreme Court on trade, it
could ultimately, intentionally or otherwise, end up indirectly
supporting the European economy.
China’s main response will be to wait and see, as it will react to
actual policy changes rather than the shifts within Congress. The

Personal ties between the two leaders appear good, but with
President Trump’s policy focus being channelled towards foreign
and trade policy, China could end up in the crosshairs again. The
trade truce is set to expire in January, and it seems like both sides
are not interested in going back to the mutually destructive peak
tariﬀ scenario we had in 2025, but it cannot be ruled out. As with
Europe, if Trump appears weakened by the outcome of the
midterms, China could sense that it is time to start looking beyond

2028, triggering a revaluation of its long-term US strategy.

達發 6526 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
光通發力，再達新高 
投資與評價 
1. 達發 3Q26 受惠光通與乙太網持續放亮，且商務耳機出貨暢旺，營收創
單季新高，獲利優於預期；預期 4Q26 光通與乙太網延續成長，淡季不
淡，營運為歷史同期新高。 
2. 單通道 100G 開始出貨，預期 27 年逐步放量，10G PON 切入 AI 資料
中心 OOB，新一代 PHY 同步貢獻，上修 26 年及 27 年營收預估，預估
26 年 EPS 21.76 元；27 年 EPS 30.18 元，營運創歷史新高。 
3. 目前達發股價為 707 元，約 23 倍 FY27E EPS ，位於近四年本益比參考
區間 20-50 倍偏下位置。考量光通高速成長，網通切入全新市場，維持
買進投資建議；惟受上下游供給緊缺，毛利率上升空間有限，將評價由
平均值 35 倍調至中間偏下 30 倍，目標價 900 元(30x FY27E EPS)。

重點更新 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，達發 3Q26 營運創近四年新高 
達發 3Q26 光通憑藉產能與交期優勢提升市占，單通道 50G 出貨優於預期，
單通道 100G 開始小量出貨，乙太網受惠低軌衛星規格升級與交換器市占提
升，藍牙受惠旺季及高階專案出貨，營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元，創單季
新高。毛利率 49.3%優於原先預估之 49.1%，費用方面控管得當，營業費用
22.87 億元(+0.6% QoQ)低於原先預估之 23.35 億元，營業利益 11.34 億元
(+20.3% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 17.0%至 10.29 億元，EPS 6.15 元，營運創
近四年單季新高。

預期光通與乙太網延續成長，達發 4Q26 營運為歷史同期新高 
達發 4Q26 提前拉貨比重有限 ， 預期藍牙受惠商務耳機量產 ， 跌幅小於往年；
光通單通道 100G 逐漸放量，乙太網 2.5G PHY 與交換器延續成長，因此上
修營收預估 5.3%至 67.80 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，淡季不淡。 預估毛利率47.8%，
營業費用 21.60 億元(-5.6% QoQ)，營業利益 10.83 億元(-4.5% QoQ)，稅
後淨利 9.88 億元(-4.1% QoQ)，EPS 5.90 元，營運為歷史同期新高。

### Page 2

報告內容 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，達發 3Q26 獲利優於預期 
達發 3Q26 光通需求強勁，在市場供給偏緊下，達發產能與交期相對穩定，單通道 50G 持續提
升市占，出貨優於預期，單通道 100G 亦開始小量出貨，50G TIA 並以獨立晶片形式出貨。乙太
網受惠低軌衛星客戶由 1G 升級至 ASP 較高的 2.5G PHY，輕管理型交換器需求增溫。寬頻以北
美成長最快，10G PON 比重逐步升高。藍牙則為消費旺季，電競及商務耳機等高階專案出貨增
加，3Q26 營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元(+23.3% YoY)，創單季新高。 
毛利率方面，DRAM 及 NOR Flash 今年漲幅達數倍，封測成本亦上升，產品漲價於 3Q26 貢獻
有限，惟產品組合優化部分抵減成本壓力，毛利率季減1.9 個百分點至 49.3%，略優於原先預估
之 49.1%。營業費用 22.87 億元(+0.6% QoQ)，低於原先預估之 23.35 億元，營業利益11.34 億
元(+20.3% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 17.0%至 10.29 億元，EPS 6.15 元，營運創近四年單季新高。

### Page 3

光通成長淡季不淡，預期達發 4Q26 營運為歷史同期新高 
達發 4Q26 有別於傳統淡季，預期光通與乙太網延續強勁成長，其中光通單通道 100G 出貨增
加，為最大成長動能，乙太網則受惠2.5G PHY 持續導入 Wi-Fi 新客戶及新一代 10G PHY 量產。
寬頻隨北美設備商產品陸續落地 ， 並切入AI 資料中心 OOB 新市場 ， 藍牙則受惠商務耳機專案量
產，高階應用占比提升，預估淡季影響優於往年，加上下半年提前拉貨占藍牙音訊及寬頻營收皆
不到一成 ， 後續客戶庫存調整壓力有限 ， 上修4Q26 營收預估 5.3%至 67.80 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，
淡季不淡。 
毛利率方面，產品價格調整預期自 4Q26 開始反映，光通占比持續提升，惟調價依客戶及產品線
逐案協商，預期無法完全反映成本漲幅，且 NOR Flash 及封測成本持續走揚，下修毛利率預估
1.3 個百分點至 47.8%(-1.5ppts QoQ)。下調營業費用 2.1%至 21.60 億元(-5.6% QoQ)，上調營
業利益預估 13.2%至 10.83 億元(-4.5% QoQ, +127.1% YoY)； 稅後淨利9.88 億元(-4.1% QoQ，
+78.0% YoY)，EPS 5.90 元，營運為歷史同期新高。

光通營收大幅成長，預期達發 26 年營運創歷史新高 
達發 26 年光通受惠全球前十大模組廠滲透率提升 ， 加上AI 資料中心光模組進入三至五年替換週
期，預期營收成長至 25 年的四倍以上，占全年營收 mid-single digit ，產品以 12 奈米單通道
50G 為主，單通道100G 則於下半年開始小量出貨。寬頻繼25 年歐洲市場放量後，26 年由北美
接棒，1~3Q26 北美營收翻倍成長，10G PON 增速領先其他寬頻產品，乙太網則受惠低軌衛星
地面設備價量齊揚，非管理型交換器海外通路市占穩健擴張，預期為成長僅次於光通的產品線，
藍牙商務耳機 26 年營收預期翻倍成長，占藍牙音訊比重由個位數提升至接近兩成，帶動ASP 提
升，上修 26 年營收預估 1.4%至 254.66 億元(+21.7% YoY)。 
毛利率方面，1H26 受惠產品組合維持於 51.9%，惟下半年上下游成本皆大幅上漲，下修毛利率
預估 0.3 個百分點至 50.1%(-1.8ppts YoY)。下調營業費用預估 1.0%至 87.82 億元(+10.4% YoY)，
費用率因營收規模放大，由 25 年的 38.0%降至 34.5%，營業利益 39.76 億元(+36.8% YoY)；預
估稅後淨利年增 25.7%至 36.45 億元，EPS 21.76 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

100G 接棒放量，預期達發 27 年營運再創歷史新高 
達發 27 年光通動能由單通道 100G 接棒，驗證進度符合預期， 1H27 逐步放量，預估於 2Q27
占光通營收一半，100G DSP ASP 為 50G 的兩倍以上，100G TIA 搭載率預期高於 50G。預期 27
年光通營收成長至 26 年的三倍，占營收比重提升至 15%。中長期則有單通道 200G 目標於 27
年底 T ape Out，並投資元澄半導體以加速模組驗證與導入。寬頻方面，25G 及 50G PON 預期
於 27 年量產，OOB 目前以較低 PON 數配置為主，配置擴大後滲透率可望提升。乙太網新一代
10G PHY 搭配 10G PON、Wi-Fi 及 5G FWA 平台，預期 2.5G 交換器出貨增加。藍牙 6 奈米新
平台預期 26 年底 T ape Out，並往 B2B 應用布局，上修 27 年營收預估 3.6%至 306.74 億元
(+20.5% YoY)。 
毛利率方面，預期產品價格調整效益 27 年逐步顯現，光通占比亦持續提升，惟使用封裝及記憶
體較多之藍牙與寬頻等產品預估合計仍占營收七至八成，且達發身為後進者，光通定價以擴大市
占為優先，下修毛利率預估 0.9 個百分點至 50.1%，與 26 年持平。研發費用隨 100G、200G 
SerDes 及 25G PON 等新產品持續投入，惟 200G SerDes IP 授權金認列為無形資產並分期攤
銷，對費用影響有限，預估營業費用97.48 億元(+11.0% YoY)，營業費用率下降至 31.8%，營業
利益 56.10 億元(+41.1% YoY)；預估稅後淨利年增 38.7%至 50.56 億元，EPS 30.18 元，營運再
創歷史新高。

100G 放量在即，達發成長動能升溫 
達發過去以消費性藍牙與家用寬頻為主，目前有線網通基礎建設營收占比已達約五成，預期 27
年持續提升，AI 資料中心相關營收比重加速擴大。高速光通、OOB 及乙太網 PHY 同步切入 AI
基礎建設，客戶由模組廠延伸至大型雲端業者，產品週期波動逐步降低，預估26 年及 27 年 EPS
分別為 21.76 元及 30.18 元，成長動能明確。 
目前達發股價為 707 元(23X FY27E EPS)，位於近四年本益比參考區間 20-50 倍下緣位置，考量
光通成長動能預期延續，OOB 架構打開 AI 資料中心新應用，維持買進投資評等。惟 27 年毛利
率預估持平於 50.1%，毛利率仍受上下游供給緊缺影響，因此將評價基礎由平均值之 35 倍調整
至中間偏下之 30 倍，目標價由 995 元(35x 修正前 FY27E EPS)調整至 900 元(30x 修正後 FY27E 
EPS)。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
達發科技股份有限公司(Airoha Technology Corp., 6526 TT) 成立於 2001 年，總部位於新竹縣
竹北市，為聯發科集團旗下 IC 設計公司。2021 年整併誠致科技、雷凌科技、絡達科技及聯發科
IoT 部門並更名為達發科技，2022 年 6 月登錄興櫃，2023 年 10 月於證交所上市。公司業務分
為有線網通基礎建設與無線邊緣 AI 兩大事業群：有線網通涵蓋固網寬頻(G-PON、10G PON)、
乙太網(PHY 與交換器晶片)及光通訊(PAM4 DSP 與 TIA)，應用由家用寬頻、低軌衛星地面設備
延伸至 AI 資料中心光通訊與帶外管理，並於 2024 年 7 月完成參與九暘私募，九暘為合併子公
司，布局 PoE 晶片；無線邊緣 AI 以藍牙音訊為主，聚焦商務、電競及聲學等中高階耳機市場，
並延伸至藍牙傳輸(電競鍵鼠、電子貨架標籤)及衛星定位(GNSS)晶片。目前有線網通基礎建設與
無線邊緣 AI 兩大事業群營收占比各約 50%。

### Page 6

ESG 政策 
達發以社會、經濟、環境及資訊揭露四大構面推動永續經營，已在員工照護、職業安全、董
事會獨立性、資訊安全及溫室氣體管理等面向建立相關制度，評估期間未發生重大人權、消
費者權益、職業災害、誠信經營及環境法規違規情事。

社會面：達發依循國際人權宣言制定人權政策並揭露執行結果，並將人權要求納入供應商評
鑑與稽核。員工管理方面，提供優於法規的休假、保險及身心健康措施，並以員工認股權與
持股信託強化人才留任；職業安全衛生管理系統已取得外部認證，並揭露工傷統計及減災目
標。另建立績效評估及能力發展計畫，設定員工多元化目標、揭露女性主管占比，並足額進
用身心障礙員工。未來可完善人權盡職調查流程，並揭露員工滿意度、訓練成效、性別薪酬
比及客戶滿意度。

經濟面：達發每年召開 2 次以上法人說明會，董事長親自出席股東常會，並揭露議案表決結
果。獨立董事席次達半數以上，設置永續發展相關委員會並由董事長擔任最高負責人，且定
期執行董事會外部績效評估。另制定反貪腐、反競爭、內線交易防範及檢舉制度，風險管理
政策經董事會通過，並設置資安專責單位、定期測試資安機制。後續可強化接班規劃、設置
提名委員會、提高任一性別董事席次至三分之一以上，並將永續績效納入高階主管與董事薪
酬。

環境面：氣候管理由董事會及管理階層監督，已揭露範疇一、二及範疇三排放，並設定量化
中長期減碳、節能及再生能源目標。達發亦制定水資源及廢棄物管理政策，揭露用水量及有
害廢棄物產出量，並將環境表現納入供應商遴選及稽核。惟尚未取得 ISO 14001 及碳盤查認
證，未來可補充 1.5°C 以下及 3°C 以上氣候情境分析、再生能源使用量及用水、廢棄物量化
減量目標。

揭露面：達發依循 GRI 準則編製永續報告書，揭露重大性分析及 SDGs 對應作為，並提供英
文版報告書及英文網站。後續可導入 SASB 對應揭露及第三方確信，建立利害關係人回饋機
制，並採用雙重重大性原則強化 ESG 資訊完整性。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投

《高利高通膨壓抑，留意日股短線震盪加大》
10月日本股市大幅震盪修正 ，符合本行下調評等之
預期。其中，日經225指數在10月初一度上破7萬點
大館，此後因AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正與長端利
率上行，全球科技股估值大幅修正，日經225指數在
10月23日盤中一度下探61,462點。
日本兩大指數在本月表現也呈現相對分化。日經225
權重權重集中於東京威力科創、愛德萬測試、軟銀集
團等半導體與 AI標的，在本波修正幅度較大；相反
的，TOPIX指數因金融、內需與防禦類股權重較高，
加上利率下行有利銀行以外的內需類股，回檔相對溫
和。也間接反映了10月的修正偏向是 「AI交易擁擠
的釋放」，而非全面性的景氣衰退定價。
債市方面，10年期日本公債殖利率在10月初一度升

至3.11%、創1999年以來新高 ，反映日本央行升
息、以及高市內閣財政擴張的雙重疑慮。
此後殖利率一路回落至 10月26日的2.82%、下行約
23個基點。這一下行幅度反映全球 AI相關信用風險
引發的避險需求。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理
5
AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正
• 10月日股受AI估值修正及長天
期利率波動影響而震盪下跌，
但修正主要集中於半導體與AI
權值股。
• TOPIX相對抗跌，顯示市場仍
未全面反映景氣衰退，而是資
金自擁擠交易部位撤出。

《日本出口維持韌性，內需動能出現疑慮》
經濟基本面部分，日本9月出口在半導體與AI相關需
求帶動下，連續13個月維持正成長 ，惟增速放緩至
16.7%、略低於預期；但進口年增卻升至18.9%，主
要因為進口能源價格大漲所致，導致貿易逆差擴大，
外需雖強卻未能轉化為淨出口貢獻。
內需與生產端則收高利高通膨壓力同步降溫。工業生
產增速自年中高點回落，製造業PMI降至七個月來最
低，整體擴張速度已低於上半年水準，細項中新訂單
與出口訂單成長同步放緩，能源與勞動成本則大幅走
升，對企業營運形成壓力。內需改善亦相當有限，零
售銷售下滑、失業率微升、求人求職比回落，反映勞
動市場略有降溫；所幸，實質薪資已連續八個月正成
長、消費者信心小幅回升。

整體而言，日本經濟仍處於「名目成長、實質停滯」
的格局，擴張主要仰賴出口，內需復甦力道偏弱，而
高油價帶來的逆差壓力與生產動能轉弱，將是後續成
長的主要考驗 。基於地緣與油價的不確定性 ，10月
日本央行暫停升息，維持利率不變。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理
5
貨幣政策正常化以及財政疑慮推升日債殖利率走高
• 日本出口持續受惠半導體與AI
需求，惟能源價格上漲推升進
口成本與貿易逆差。工業生產、
PMI及勞動市場同步降溫，經
濟仍維持「出口強、內需弱」
格局，成長動能面臨考驗。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《日銀暫停升息，12月升息預期支撐利率高檔》
日本央行已於 9月會期將政策利率上調 25個基點至
1.25%，為1995年以來最高 ，也是三個月內第二
次、1990年以來最快的升息節奏。
隨著近期經濟數據 ，10月份日央如預期暫停升息一
碼，會議同步公布《經濟與物價形勢展望報告》，根
據央行經濟預測顯示潛在通膨「已大致達到2%」。
配合植田和男在10月16日記者會曾表示，若基礎通
膨進一步上升將是 「無法接受」的，並在10月19日
國會答詢時表示潛在通膨非常接近2%目標。
加以隨著國際油價再度上破110美元大關，短期政策
重點從「對抗通縮 」轉為「防止通膨超標 」，是以
12月再度升息一碼的機率已大幅提高。
另一方，高市內閣的食品消費稅減稅與國防支出增

加，使市場對財源安排存疑 。不過，10月19日高市
在國會承諾在下一財年預算中妥善管控國債發行規
模，使近期長端利率有所回落 。但12月下一財年預
算與國債發行計畫公布時，財政紀律的可信度將再受
檢驗，預期短線利率上行趨緩，維持高檔盤整。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理
5

上漲與通膨接近目標，提高年
底再升息機率。
• 另一方面，財政擴張與國債供
給疑慮仍在，預期長天期利率
維持高檔震盪。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《盤勢觀點-資金輪動加速，防禦成長族群接棒》
10月日本股市震盪 、類股表現分化 。市場資金明顯
由上半年漲幅龐大的AI硬體與半導體族群撤出，轉向
軟體服務、醫療保健及內需消費等防禦成長型產業。
其中，東京威力科創、SCREEN、瑞薩電子等指標股
普遍出現雙位數跌幅。相較之下，軟體服務類股大漲
17.9%，BayCurrent、SoftBank等個股表現突出。醫
藥生技、消費零售及娛樂觀光相關族群亦受惠於防禦
需求與內需消費韌性而表現相對亦強勢。值得注意的
是，銀行與非銀行金融股同步走弱，多家大型金融機
構跌幅介於8%至15%，顯示市場擔憂金融條件收緊
可能壓抑經濟活動與資本市場表現。
整體而言，10月日股市場風格由 「AI硬體主導」轉

向「防禦成長與內需消費主導」，同時對經濟基本面
疑慮小幅升溫 ，8~9月相對強勢的金融出現回調壓
力。展望未來，短期盤勢仍受利匯率壓力，以及科技
股評價調整影響 ，加以1H27預估日經225指數企業
獲利年增率轉為負成長，因此本行維持9月以來下調
日股至中性的看法不變，待國際油價回落以及年底年
度預算案底定後，可伺機布局。
重點摘要
5

禦成長族群。半導體、金融與
工業設備股表現疲弱，反映投
資人開始關注高利率環境及企
業獲利成長放緩風險。

《盤勢觀點-澳洲景氣放緩，股匯市轉向盤整》
隨著國際油價維持衝高，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至 4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。但在高利率與高通膨
壓力持續積累下對景氣負面壓力逐漸加大。
資料顯示，澳洲9月失業率升至4.72%，已連續兩個
月 落 在 澳 央 主 席 認 為 可 有 效 壓 抑 通 膨 的 失 業 率
4.5~5.0%區間；而通膨方面 ，9月份澳洲 CPI雖由
4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以住房通
膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本輪升息
暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不
振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有

限，加以預期聯準會在 12月再度升息 ，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整。
綜上，持續升息後澳洲基本面轉弱，但高油價壓力又
迫使央行貨幣政策難以轉向寬鬆，加以企業獲利未見
改善動能，澳洲股市難以樂觀，本行維持中性展望。
重點摘要
• 高油價迫使澳洲央行維持偏
鷹態度 ，但高利率已逐步壓
抑就業與內需。
• 中國需求偏弱限制出口前景 ，
加上美澳利差優勢收斂 ，預
期澳幣短期維持區間震盪 ，
澳股展望偏中性。
澳幣期貨部位轉向淨空方

Document 519: バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

Source PDF: all report pdf/バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 6 日

MURC Focus

バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

～10 月末発表の秋季予算案で具体的な財源を示せるか～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 伝統的な財政拡張を訴えるバーナム新政権 
英国では、9 月 27 日から 30 日にかけて、北西部の主要都市リバプールで与党である労働党の党大会が開催
された。7 月 20 日に就任したアンディ・バーナム首相にとって初の党大会となると同時に、新政権の財政運営方
針が示されるという点で、この党大会は非常に高い注目を浴びた。この場で示されたのは、中道左派である労働
党らしい、伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。

具体的には、バーナム 新政権の関係閣僚らは、政府調達（政府が民間からモノやサービスを購入すること）の
増強による景気浮揚策を主張した。例えばジョン・ヒーリー財務相は 28 日の党大会で、スコットランドのクライド海
軍基地に浮きドック 3 基を建設する他、海洋調査船を建造することに数十億ポンドを投資する計画を発表した。
いずれも国内の業者に発注することで雇用や所得につなげる方針である。

また労働党大会に先立つ 26 日、バーナム新政権は、新築住宅を初めて購入する国民向けに新たな住宅購
入支援策を導入すると発表した。保守党政権下の 2013 年から 2023 年にかけて実施された“Help to Buy”と似
たスキームであり、購入者は購入価格の 2.5%に相当する頭金を支払えば、政府が購入価格の 20%までを融資
するというものである。

このように、バーナム新政権が目指す方針は、労働党に伝統的な“大きな政府”による財政拡張路線を反映し
ている。そうした点で、バーナム新政権の方針はキア・スターマー前政権とはあまり変化がない。一方で、新政権
が示した財政拡張路線が金融市場の評価を得るためには、財源を明示する必要がある。10 月 28 日に発表する
秋季予算案で、金融市場が納得する内容を示せるかが鍵を握る。

〇9月末にリバプールで開催された労働党の党大会で、バーナム新政権の財政運営方針が示されたが 、それ
は中道左派である労働党らしい伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。 
〇一方、金融市場 では、それまではバーナム 人気によって労働党 の政権運営が安定するとの思惑から“英国
買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。 
〇増税は有権者の反発を、国債発行が投資家の反発を生むと予想される中、秋季予算で財政拡張の財源の
筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。

### Page 2

2. 財政拡張を続けてきた近年の英国 
バーナム新政権は財政拡張に活路を 見いだそうとしているが、これまでも英国の政権は財政拡張に臨んでき
た（図表 1）。例えば英国の実質 GDP（国内総生産）に占める公需（＝政府支出＋公的固定資本形成）は、保守
党政権（テリーザ・メイ元首相）期の 2018 年後半に 21.5%を下回るまで低下したが、以降は上昇し、直近 2026
年 4～6 月時点で 24.4%となっている（図表 1）。

この間、2020 年 1～3 月期に生じたコロナショック後の公需の減退も限定的にとどまっており、結局は公需が
GDP に占める割合は“V 字回復”することとなった。労働党が政権を奪取したのは 2024 年 7 月の総選挙だが、
それ以前の保守党政権下でも、英国は事実上、財政拡張を続けていたことになる。その結果、公的債務残高の
対 GDP 比率も、一時は高インフレのために低下したが、再び上昇した（図表 2）。

このように近年の英国は、保革の立場を問わず財政拡張を続けてきた。しかし、EU 離脱やエネルギー不安に
伴う供給制約の高まりの中でそうした手法を取り続けたため 、英国はスタグフレーション（景気停滞と物価高進の
併存）に突入した。その改善のために必要な処方箋は、規制緩和を通じた供給の刺激である。しかし、それは“小
さな政府”を追求することにもなるため、中道左派として“大きな政府”を目指す労働党は容認できない。

バーナム新首相は地方の主導の経済成長を訴え、そうした意味での分権化に意欲的と考えられる。 ただし労
働党としては、公需による民需の刺激を通じた景気浮揚を引き続き志向していることが、秋の党大会で示された
形である。一方、金融市場では、バーナム新首相就任直後は、首相の人気に支えられて新政権の運営が安定す
るとの思惑から“英国買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。

### Page 3

3. 財源なき財政拡張で信用を失うリスク 
スターマー前政権もまた、財政拡張路線による景気浮揚を目指した が、同時にそれは、供給の底上げを狙うも
のでもあった。老朽インフラ（鉄道や水道など）の再開発はその最たるものである。またスターマー前政権は、財政
規律にも配慮し、財源を国債発行よりも増税でカバーし、 財政拡張策の整合性を取ろう とした。財源を示したこと
を投資家は評価したが、増税に有権者が反発したため、結局、スターマー前政権は短命に終わった。

スターマー前政権下で低迷した労働党の支持率は、バーナム人気に支えられ“V 字回復”し、右派の改革党
（UK Reform ）と肉薄するに至る（図表 3）。労働党としては打ち出した目玉政策を着実に遂行し、有権者の支持
をさらに確たるものにしたいところである。とはいえ、そうした目玉政策は財政拡張を伴うものであるため、財源をど
う確保するか明確な筋道を示さなければ、金融市場の動揺を誘う。

すでに英国の長期金利（10 年国債流通利回り）は、グローバルなインフレ加速懸念に伴う世界的な金利上昇
の波に飲まれ、上昇が続いている（図表 4）。金融市場は財政拡張に対して神経を尖らせているが、秋季予算で
明確な財源が示さなければ、それを嫌気した投資家が英国債（ギルト債）の売却を進め、金利が一段と上昇し、
場合によってポンド安と株安の“トリプル安”を引き起こす可能性もある。

財政拡張の財源を増税で賄おうとすると、スターマー前政権のように有権者の支持を失いかねない。 一方、国
債発行で賄おうとすると、長期金利の押し上げ圧力になる。ゆえに、歳出の見直しで財源を賄おうとするだろうが、
簡単ではないため、具体的な筋道を示せなければ、結局は金利の上昇につながる。秋季予算で財政拡張の財
源の筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。

Document 583: 内外経済ウォッチ『欧州～フランスの財政再建を阻む政治の壁～』(2026年11月号)

Source PDF: all report pdf/内外経済ウォッチ『欧州～フランスの財政再建を阻む政治の壁～』(2026年11月号).pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

欧州
～フランスの財政再建を阻む政治の壁～
フランスの財政不安が広がっている。 10月に発表した
2027年予算案では、 2026年に国内総生産 （Ｇ Ｄ Ｐ ） 比
5.4％に達する見込みの財政赤字を、 2027年に5.0％へ
縮小する方針を掲げた。 政府はこう した計画を達成する
ため、 年金の物価スライ ドの部分停止や税制優遇の縮小
を盛り込んだが、 年金受給者に負担を求める財政再建策
に野党勢が猛反発しており、 予算協議が難航している。
2024年の国民議会 （下院） 選挙以降、 安定多数を持
つ政治勢力がなく、 政府は予算協議のたびに野党との
妥協を迫られてきた。 昨年は年内に予算を成立できず、
年明け後に年金改革の棚上げを条件に野党の協力を取
り付けた。 一昨年は予算協議の難航で政権が崩壊した。
政治の混乱と財政再建の遅れへの警戒から、 フランスの
10年物国債利回りは2002年以来の高水準に上昇して
いる。
本格的な財政再建には年金や医療、 地方財政、 公務員
給与などへの切り込みが避けられない。 だが、 年金給付

や医療費の抑制には国民や労働組合が反発し、 地方への
支出削減には自治体が抵抗する。 フランスは既に税 ・ 社
会保険料負担が重く、 増税にも限界がある。 企業増税は
競争力の低下を招き、 富裕層課税で巨額の赤字を埋める
ことも難しい。 財政再建には幅広い層への負担を伴う
が、 現在の政治環境では合意形成が極めて難しい。
進まない財政再建、 予算協議は難航
財政再建をさらに難し く しているのが、 2027年春に
迫る大統領選挙だ。 選挙を前に、 与野党とも有権者に痛
みを求める政策を支持しにくい。 大統領選挙の世論調査
は、 極右政党 ・ 国民連合 （ Ｒ Ｎ） のマリーヌ ・ ルペン氏が圧
倒的にリー ド している。 Ｒ Ｎは歳出削減を通じて財政再建
に取り組む方針を示唆しているが、 その具体策には欧州
連合 （ Ｅ Ｕ） への拠出金削減など、 実効性が疑われる内容
も含まれる。 大統領選後の財政再建の行方や欧州連合
（ Ｅ Ｕ） との関係悪化も不安視される。
フランスの財政不安が他の欧州諸国に広がり、 2010

年代前半の欧州債務危機のような深刻な危機に発展す
る可能性は今のところ低い。 フランスはユーロ圏有数の
経済規模と厚みのある国債市場を持ち、 市場での資金
調達から締め出されているわけではない。 債務危機後
の欧州は、 金融安全網の整備や危機の再発防止策など
を整備してきた。
フランスの苦境は、 日本にと っても対岸の火事ではな
い。 日本も高齢化に伴う社会保障費の増加と巨額の政
府債務を抱え、 金利上昇が続けば利払い費が膨らむ。 両
国の事情は異なるが、 財政再建の遅れが将来の政策余
力を狭め、 市場の信任を損ないかねない点は共通する。
日本にと っても、 財政健全化と政治的合意形成をどう両
立するかは避けて通れない課題だ。
大統領選挙で高まる不透明感
経済調査部　首席エコノミス ト　田中 理（たなか おさむ）
資料1 フランスの財政収支の対Ｇ Ｄ Ｐ 比率

Document 593: 凱基投顧_3036 文曄_許芝瑄_20261105

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_3036 文曄_許芝瑄_20261105.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  通路 ‧ 台灣

文曄 
(3036.TW /3036 TT)

AI 需求持續，然獲利成長有限 
重要訊息 
文曄召開法說會，3Q26 獲利優於預期，4 Q26 持續成長。惟 2027 年 EPS 將
受毛利率下滑、財務成本提升及股本擴大影響，成長有限。  
評論及分析  
3Q26 獲利優於預期， 4Q26 持續季增。文曄 3Q26 營收季增 1.1%，年增
81.6%，優於預期。其中，資料中心業務受出貨節奏影響季減 4.9%，工業
及車用需求則優於預期，Future 亦延續上半年復甦趨勢，營收年增逾 40%。
受惠高毛利的工業、車用及 Future 營收占比提升，毛利率季增至 3.6%，營
業費用率則大致持平。不過，業務成長帶動營運資金需求增加以及利率環境
變化，財務成本 19.4 億元高於原先預估的 17-18 億元，單季 EPS 為 7.97
元。展望 4Q26，儘管工業、車用、 PC 及消費性電子進入季節性淡季，公司
預期資料中心及通訊業務營收將呈高雙位數季增，成為推升整體營運的主要
動能。然而，產品組合變化及財務成本升至 26-28 億元將影響獲利增幅，凱
基預估 4Q26 營收及獲利將季增 26.8%及 15.4%，EPS 為 8.85 元。 
2027 年 AI 需求延續，惟獲利增幅放緩，股本擴大進一步壓抑 EPS 成長。展
望 2027 年，管理層對資料中心與通訊業務維持樂觀看法，認為 AI 為長期性
成長，此外，工業與車用已走出去庫存階段，公司亦正面看待 2027 年需
求。凱基預估文曄 2027 年營收將年增 37.2%。然而，產品組合使毛利率進
一步下滑，加之升息環境推升財務成本，預估獲利年增 25.1%，為 2024 年
以來首次獲利 年增率低於營收。此外，發行 GDR 使得股本擴大，稀釋效應
將於 2027 全年反映，預估 2027 年 EPS 僅達 36.24 元，年增 21.3%。 
投資建議  
儘管 AI 相關需求仍支撐文曄營收成長，惟毛利率下滑、 財務成本增加及股本
擴大使 2027 年 EPS 成長有限。凱基認為，獲利年增率大幅下滑加之升息環境
不利於通路業者，因此我們將目標本益比調降至 6 倍，相當於歷史週期平均
值減一個標準差，目標價調降至 215元，投資評等調降至「持有」。  
投資風險 
總體經濟環境劣化 。

文曄科技創立於 1993 年，並於 2002 年上櫃，已成為亞洲領先
大半導體通路商，負責提供高品質與附加價值的業務給半導體供
應商與下游客戶。公司主要聚焦 於亞洲市場，2025 年營收佔比
方面，中國地區佔 23.8%，美國地區佔 36.3%，台灣及其他地
區，分別貢獻 25.7%與 14.2%。
